(**Recut 2026-08-15 on `af003548`:** the leg used to require `aging_reval_cancels > 0` specifically; on a post-Fix-77 build the dead rails are removed by the live-count stale path and the week-30 sweep, so `aging_reval_cancels` legitimately reads 0 while the queue empties — `sweep_n` 13 + `stale_n` 3 carried the cancels. The claim is unchanged — the fossils are cancelled, not frozen — only the counter naming was too narrow.) **No factories may be burned on them in the meantime** — Fix 34's controller test must hold, so `assigned_factories` on a hostile-target project stays 0.
  3. **Redirect leg (the point).** After the queue clears, the capitulated country queues projects on soil it **does** control — for FRA, any `wa_ai_pc_target_state` in the Maghreb (458, 459, 460, 461, 462) or its other held territory. A queue that empties and then stays empty while the country holds 49 states is a partial pass: report it, because it means the `WA_AI_priority_construction_strategies` dispatcher is still blocked by something else (most likely `WA_AI_PC_can_afford_project`).
- **Probe:** `savegame.py var FRA "^wa_ai_pc_"` across ≥3 consecutive monthly saves — the diff between saves *is* the measurement, so pass consecutive months, not distant anchors. Pair with `savegame.py tlm FRA --match pc_aging`. Capitulation is readable at `capitulated=yes` in the country's `diplomacy={}` sub-block and is **written only when true**. **`surrender_progress` is NOT serialized** — do not plan any leg around reading it. No new telemetry ships with this fix; the probe is save-native plus the existing Fix 41 counters, so there is **no `wa_tlm_version` floor** and no absence contract beyond "the build predates Fix 75", which the byte-identical signature itself reveals.
- **Threshold:** 3 (narrow — every leg is a discrete "did these variables move / did this state id appear" fact off one sweep).
- **Deliberately NOT changed, and the reason leg 3 may look weak:** Fix 75 removes *capitulation* as a disqualifier; it does not lower the capability bars. For any capitulated country the binding term is the surrender gate, whose escape hatch demands 75 civs — so FRA (21) still fails `WA_AI_PC_country_can_build_own_logistics` and **still will not lay its own railways**. That is the intended outcome: the Tunisian rail comes from Fix 74's ally leg, which fires for exactly the countries that fail here. Whether `surrender_progress` should gate this at all is R27's question, and relaxing that hatch would touch every losing country in every campaign — a far wider blast radius than this fix. If a campaign shows a capitulated major with an intact war economy (>75 civs, >20 states) still locked out, that is R27, not this item.
- **Regression watch, in priority order:**
  - **Newly-iterated population.** Every capitulated AI tag now runs `WA_AI_PC_assign_factories` and `WA_AI_PC_update_project_progress` weekly. `exists = yes` excludes annexed corpses; if a campaign shows PC variables appearing on a non-existent tag, that term is not doing its job.
  - **A capitulated country must not gain posture, lend-lease or a training modifier.** Those three kept the gate. `wa_ai_military_posture` appearing on a capitulated tag is an outright FAIL of the split, not a widening.
  - **Trigger drift is now impossible by construction** — `WA_AI_PC_country_cannot_build_own_logistics` is defined as the strict `NOT` of the eligibility trigger, and the on_action calls that same trigger. This retires the "eligibility literals are triplicated" watch item R46 carried.
- **Streak:** 2
- **Status:** PASSED
- **History:**
  - 2026-08-14 · `be18f9c7` · PASSED — **All three legs, on the first test of Fix 75.** Leg 1 PASS - FRA is `capitulated=yes` from **1940.7.8** through at least 1945.6 while owning 57-59 states, controlling 48-52 and holding 21-26 civ factories, and its PC block is **not** byte-identical: `wa_ai_pc_stall_weeks^0` advances every month (160 @1943.6 -> 190 @1944.1 -> 203 @1944.3), i.e. the weekly PC block now runs on a capitulated tag. Leg 2 PASS - the 36-project fossil (frozen 1940.9->1944.3, `assigned_factories_total = 0`, 10 rails aimed at GER-held states) is **cancelled, not merely frozen**: the queue collapses 36 -> 1 at 1944.5 and `wa_tlm_pc_aging_reval_cancels` on FRA reads **18 (1944.6) -> 46 (1945.6) -> 76 (1946.7)**; no factories burned meanwhile (`assigned_factories^0/^1/^4 = 0`, `progress^0` pinned at 5802.668 for the whole 43-month freeze - the Fix 34 controller test holds). Leg 3 PASS - FRA re-queues and funds: 1944.5 one project with 6 factories, 1944.8 **12 projects**, 1944.10 **32**, 1945.6 **39**, 1946.7 4 projects with **33 assigned factories**. Not the "empties and stays empty" partial pass. Regression watch clean - `wa_ai_military_posture` on FRA is 0 pre-capitulation and 0 throughout capitulation, becoming 1 only at 1946.7 after liberation, and `lend_lease_targets^num = 0` while capitulated. The split held.
  - 2026-08-15 · `af003548` · PASSED — FRA capitulated 1940.7.29 while holding territory; PC block **not** byte-identical month to month (hydro_steel stall 14 → 18 → 22 → 27 → 79); the 14-project fossil **CANCELLED** (queue 14 → 1 by 1944.3, `wa_tlm_pc_sweep_n` 13 + `stale_n` 3), zero factories burned; re-queues on liberated soil at 1946.5 (10 projects / 30 factories / 9 rails, `pc_built_n` 32 → 35). Probe defect found and recut above: leg 2 as written required `aging_reval_cancels > 0` and it reads 0 — the cancels landed in `sweep_n`/`stale_n` on this post-Fix-77 build. Streak 2 of 3.

### R48. Synthetic refineries are built BEFORE the rubber shortage, not two years after it (Fix 76)

- **Opened 2026-08-14** from campaign `be18f9c7` (BHU observer, 126 monthly saves 1936.2–1946.7, build `d683fb022`), user report: the AI should build synthetic refineries in advance of the shortage rather than reacting once it is already in a permanent deficit, USA named as the case.
- **The measurement that opened it, and the target correction it forced.** The USA is **never** in an effective deficit on bauxite, iron, coal or oil — all four EFFECTIVE balances (`to_use[0] + to_use[2]`, the column mod script reads as `resource@X`) are positive in all 12 sampled saves, bauxite bottoming at +69 and running +10 059 from 1943.6, coal never below +1 000. The one raw resource the USA is genuinely short of is **rubber** — which is exactly what a `synthetic_refinery` makes (`local_resources_rubber = 5`, plus `fuel_gain_from_states = 2.0`). Timeline: `wa_ai_needs_rubber = 3` from **1938.6**; imports 10 → 60 (1940.6) → 86 (1941.6) → **0 (1942.6)**; rubber EFFECTIVE −168 (1942.6) → −417 → −472 → −614 (1946.7); **first `synthetic_refinery` level 1944.6** — 24 months after the deficit opened and 72 after the warning signal latched — reaching 41 levels (205 rubber/day) only by 1946.6, still against −387.
- **Root cause:** every synthetic-refinery build path in the mod required `resource@rubber < 0`, i.e. the deficit had to already exist. An 18 000-IC building yielding 5 rubber/level cannot be raised in useful quantity on that schedule. The leading signal — `resource_imported@rubber`, the volume covered from someone else's territory and therefore the volume a war removes — was available and healthy-balance-compatible from 1940.6, and no path read it. The reactive `WA_AI_C.10` branch additionally carries a hard `date > 1941.1.1` (a story gate; **left in place by Fix 76 deliberately** — see the "not changed" note below).
- **Fix under test:** **Fix 76** (commit `<FILL AT COMMIT>`). New trigger `WA_AI_CONSTRUCTION_should_pre_build_synth_rubber` in `common/scripted_triggers/WA_AI_CONSTRUCTION_triggers.txt` (tech + `resource_consumed@rubber > 9` + **`resource@rubber > -1`** + `resource_imported@rubber > 20` + the coal guard `resource@coal > 50` / `resource_imported@coal < 20` + `WA_AI_PC_has_project_civs_20` + capitulation guards). Consumed by ONE strictly-additive `else_if`: the `WA_AI_C.10` refinery block (`events/WA_AI_construction.txt`, one `WA_AI_queue_REF`). `WA_AI_build_refinery_resource_shortage_rubber` is code-unchanged — it carries only a comment block recording the removed prototype host. New probe family `WA_TLM_r48_prebuild_synth_n` / `_first_t` / `_last_t`, `wa_tlm_version` **13**.
- **ONE host, after measuring who can actually use the lane.** A second host in the priority-construction lane was prototyped and **removed before shipping**. Its premise — *"ENG's C.10 is silent (R24), so ENG needs a PC host"* — argued reachability and never checked eligibility. Qualifying tags (rubber balance ≥ 0 **and** imports > 20 **and** coal guard satisfied, any sampled date): **USA** (1940.6, 1941.6), **JAP** (1937.6–1940.6), **SOV** (1938.6–1941.12). **ENG never** — it owns Malaya, so it imports 0 rubber for the whole 1936–1941 positive-balance stretch, and every date it does import already has a negative balance. **GER never** — imports 0 from 1938 on, because it is already pre-built by hand (the `original_tag = GER` startup block adds 22 refinery levels, park 4 → 36 by 1940.6; **Fix 76 is the generic form of that**). **ITA never** — clears the rubber terms three times, rejected by the coal guard every time (imports 700–1600 coal). So every qualifying tag either reaches C.10 (USA) or already builds unaided (JAP 0→4, SOV 13→55). Separately, `wa_ai_overext_dbg_mic_redirects_refinery` is **absent on all five majors at all 26 sampled dates** — Fix 39's refinery leg has never executed once and is not counted on.
- **RESOLVED 2026-08-15 by Fix 80 — the blocking defect below is fixed and this item is fully scoreable, USA included.** The dispatcher exclusion (inherited from Expert AI, predating the EAI→WA rename `bd11fde6b`, never justified in any WA commit) now carries an escape: it still blocks `WA_AI_C.10` for ENG before 1942.1.1 and USA before 1942.6.1, **unless `WA_AI_CONSTRUCTION_should_pre_build_synth_rubber = yes`**. The escape therefore opens only on days the pre-build lane wants to build. Exposure was measured branch by branch on `be18f9c7` rather than assumed: of C.10's 16 branches, the reactive refinery is mutually exclusive by construction, steel and aluminium stay shut on their own bars (USA steel effective **+392** at 1940.6 and **+31** at 1941.6 against `< -20`; aluminium **+7** and **+17**), AA/air-base/fuel-silo are capability-gated with the silo excluding USA by name, and the generic tail is an **empty `else_if`** for GER/ITA/USA/JAP. **Exactly one branch newly opens** — resource-state infrastructure — and the USA carries `wa_ai_needs_aluminium = 3` from 1940, so that is suppressed-desirable behaviour rather than a regression. ENG never satisfies the pre-build trigger (owns Malaya, imports 0 rubber), so the escape never opens for it and its tag-specific programme stays gated. Write-up: `documentation/WA_SYNTHETIC_RUBBER_PREBUILD.md` §3.4b–3.4c. **Watch on the next campaign:** if `wa_tlm_r48_prebuild_synth_n` is still 0 for the USA, the cause is no longer the dispatcher — re-read the trigger's own terms.
- **RE-DIAGNOSED 2026-08-15 on `af003548` (v15, Fix 80 IN) — still `_n = 0` on every major, and the cause WAS still the dispatcher: Fix 80's escape sat one level too low.** The trigger's own terms were re-read against the saves and were ALL true for the USA at 1941.9 and 1942.1 (rubber EFFECTIVE +26.7 / +10.0, imported 106.7 / 92.0, coal +8 415 / +7 007 with 0 coal imports, `synth_oil_experiments` level 1 since 1936.6.19, `WA_AI_PC_has_project_civs_20` floored), and the `WA_AI_construction_timer` flag was ABSENT at both dates — so the daily gate was false on its one remaining term, `num_of_available_civilian_factories > 1`, the **idle-civ throttle** that Fix 80's OR was nested *under*, not beside. **Fix 84** moves the escape up: `OR = { num_of_available_civilian_factories > 1  WA_AI_CONSTRUCTION_should_pre_build_synth_rubber = yes }` (`common/on_actions/WA_AI_misc_on_actions.txt`), so the pre-build trigger bypasses both the idle throttle and the date exclusion; every other C.10 branch keeps the throttle. Bounded by the trigger (pre-shortage only, self-limiting import term, coal guard, civs_20 floor) and by C.10's 75–1000-day re-arm timer. Two dispatch counters were added so the next silence is attributable: `wa_tlm_r48_c10_fire_n` (every C.10 dispatch) and `wa_tlm_r48_c10_escape_n` / `_last_t` (dispatches with no idle civ = the escape, by construction). **Read them first on the next campaign:** `escape_n = 0` → the trigger, not the gate; `escape_n > 0` with `prebuild_synth_n = 0` → C.10 was reached and `WA_AI_queue_REF` found no free core site (`any_of_scopes` precondition, `events/WA_AI_construction.txt`). Absence contract for the new counters: `wa_tlm_version < 17` = NOT CHECKED (they ship in the v17 build alongside Fix 88).
- **(SUPERSEDED by Fix 80, kept for the diagnosis) BLOCKING DEFECT FOUND IN REVIEW 2026-08-15 — Fix 76 is INERT for the USA and for ENG. This was resolved the same day - see the entry above.** The proactive lane's only host is `WA_AI_C.10`, and the daily dispatcher (`common/on_actions/WA_AI_misc_on_actions.txt:67-87`) refuses to fire that event for `tag = USA` while `date < 1942.6.1`, and for `tag = ENG` while `date < 1942.1.1`. Measured on `be18f9c7`: the trigger is armed at 1940.6 (rubber EFFECTIVE **+9.0**, imported 60) and 1941.6 (**+4.0**, imported 86) — both months in which C.10 cannot fire — and at 1942.6, the first month it can, the balance is **−168.0** with imports at **0**, so both the balance term and the import term are already false and never reopen. `wa_tlm_r48_prebuild_synth_n` will therefore read **0** for the USA in every campaign, and that 0 is a dispatcher exclusion, not a behavioural finding. This item's qualification table verified the trigger's terms against saves but never checked whether the host event fires. Options, all design decisions: (a) hoist the proactive lane into its own host, (b) narrow the dispatcher exclusion so it gates only C.10's other branches, (c) accept the lane as JAP/SOV-only and re-aim the fix. Full write-up: `documentation/WA_SYNTHETIC_RUBBER_PREBUILD.md` §3.4b. **Until then, legs 1 and 2 are scoreable only on JAP and SOV.**
- **Pass, three legs:**
  1. **Timing leg (the fix itself).** On at least one rubber-importing AI major, `wa_tlm_r48_prebuild_synth_first_t` (decoded) lands **strictly before** that country's rubber EFFECTIVE balance first goes negative. On a `be18f9c7`-shaped run that is the USA and the bar is `_first_t` < 1942.6. `_n = 0` with `_last_t = 0` on every major = the lane never armed (diagnose the trigger, do not re-tune it).
  2. **Behavioural leg (the point).** The USA (or the equivalent import-dependent major) holds `synthetic_refinery` levels **> 0 before its rubber balance turns negative**, and its level count at the first negative-balance save exceeds the `be18f9c7` baseline of **0**. Park read via `savegame.py buildings <TAG> <saves> --match refinery`.
  3. **Exclusivity leg (the no-regression guarantee).** The proactive counter must never move while the country is in deficit — the trigger's `resource@rubber > -1` term makes that structural, so a `_last_t` that keeps advancing after the balance has gone permanently negative means the two lanes are double-queueing and is an outright FAIL, not a widening.
- **Probe:** `savegame.py tlm USA <saves> --match r48` and the same for ENG/GER/JAP; pair with `savegame.py resources USA <saves>` (read the **EFFECTIVE** column, not `produced` and not `net`) and `savegame.py buildings USA <saves> --match refinery`. Cross-check the arming date against `savegame.py var USA "^wa_ai_needs_rubber"`. **Absence contract:** `wa_tlm_version < 13` = pre-Fix-76 build, probe **VOID, never FAILED**.
- **Threshold:** 3 (narrow — every leg is a discrete "did this stamp land before this date / is this level count above zero" fact off one sweep).
- **Deliberately NOT changed, and why:**
  - **The `date > 1941.1.1` on the reactive C.10 branch stays.** Removing it would widen the *reactive* lane for every AI in the game from 1936 — a far larger blast radius than this fix, and unrelated to the pre-build question. The proactive lane carries no date at all, which is what closes the ahistorical gap that mattered (a crisis arriving in 1938 or 1944 is served on the same terms). If a campaign shows a country in a genuine pre-1941 rubber deficit with high consumption and no build, that is this note, not a Fix 76 failure — report it and open the question separately.
  - **`WA_AI_PC_active_nonrail_projects < 5` stays.** It is shared by every PC strategy and bounds queue growth for every country. The USA's radar-dominated queue choking its own refinery lane (5 / 8 / 6 non-rail projects at the three sampled dates) is a real finding but a separate one — and it is moot for this fix, which no longer touches priority construction at all.
  - **No capacity counter.** The lane is self-limiting through the engine's own trade response (domestic synthetic output rises → the trade AI buys less → `resource_imported@rubber` falls below the floor → the trigger closes). Summing `synthetic_refinery` over every owned state on a 2-day pulse would cost more than the feedback loop it replaced.
- **Regression watch, in priority order:**
  - **Coal.** 60 coal per level, and WA's `BASE_FACTORY_SPEED = 0.0` makes a coal deficit catastrophic rather than tolerable. The guard is the same two terms the reactive lane already used, but a pre-build spends coal *earlier*. Watch `resource@coal` EFFECTIVE on any country whose `r48_prebuild_synth_n` is large; a major going coal-negative with a non-zero counter is a FAIL of the guard.
  - **Speculative IC spend.** 18 000 IC per level, bought before the shortage exists. One level per firing (against the reactive branch's two) and C.10's own 75–1000 day re-arm timer are the cadence bound. A country with `r48_prebuild_synth_n` in the dozens *and* a stalled civilian-factory count is the failure shape to look for.
  - **`break` as shared temp state — closed, but fragile by nature.** `WA_AI_queue_REF` uses `break` as its loop break variable *and* reads it after the loop, never zeroing on entry. The proactive branch saves the incoming value, zeroes, calls, tests, and **restores** — so it is invisible downstream. Any future edit that drops the restore reintroduces a cross-block coupling that no error message will report.
  - **No demolition on a speculative build.** `WA_AI_queue_REF` falls through to a MIC-replacement leg (removes an `arms_factory` to free a slot) when its loop finds nothing. The proactive branch carries an `any_of_scopes` free-core-site precondition to stay out of it. If a campaign shows a qualifying country's `arms_factory` count dropping in step with `r48_prebuild_synth_n`, that precondition is not holding.
- **Streak:** 0
- **Status:** FAILED
- **History:**
  - 2026-08-16 · `af003548` **local probe of Fix 84** (`GER_1941_06_10_01.hoi4` / `BHU_1941_06_14_15.hoi4`, resumed ~1940.7 on v16, run to 1941.6) · **NOT EXERCISED — the timer, not the gate.** `wa_tlm_r48_c10_fire_n = 0`, `_escape_n = 0`, `prebuild_synth_n = 0` on USA: C.10 never fired at all in the branch because `WA_AI_construction_timer` was set **1940.7.7.1** (inherited from the cloud path) and C.10's own re-arm timer is `clamp(5000 × (1 − mod) / num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects, 75, 1000)` days — with the engine's available-for-projects reading near 0 under a saturated queue + PC share, the timer pins at **1 000 days**, i.e. C.10 fires about once every 2.7 years for exactly the majors this lane is for (cloud: flag present 1936.1→1941.1, absent 1941.9/1942.1, set again 1942.3.3 and 1943.4.28). USA at 1941.6: rubber EFFECTIVE **+2.0**, imports 100–110, still exporting 50, **zero synthetic refineries**. Two consequences: (1) re-run the probe from **`1941.9_Sep.hoi4`** (flag ABSENT there — C.10 fires day 1 through the escape if Fix 84 works); (2) the timer divisor is the same unfloored engine token Fix 40 already convicted — a candidate Fix 87 (floor the divisor with the `civs_20`-style floor) once (1) says the escape itself works. Streak untouched.
  - NOT YET TESTED — fix postdates every campaign in the registry (`be18f9c7` is the campaign that motivated it, on build `d683fb022`).
  - 2026-08-15 · `af003548` · **FAILED — first test of Fixes 76 + 80, and the lane NEVER ARMED.** Build IN (`wa_tlm_version = 15`, not void). `wa_tlm_r48_prebuild_synth_n = 0` with `_first_t = _last_t = 0` on **USA/ENG/GER/JAP/SOV/ITA** — every major, including the JAP/SOV cases the qualification table said would qualify unaided. USA `synthetic_refinery` = 0 at every save through 1943.5, first level **1943.6** (12 months earlier than `be18f9c7`, but via the reactive C.10 branch, not the lane); rubber EFFECTIVE +1.0 (1939.1) → −25 (1940.6) → +7.7 (1941.6) → **−41.9 (1942.6, permanent)** → −522 (1943.6) → −536 (1945.1) → −370 → −234 → **−142 (1946.5)**; park **74 levels**, produced 1 543 at 1946.5 (vs 41 levels / −614 last time — a 4.3× better end-state, reactively). Legs 1 and 2 FAIL; leg 3 vacuous. **Diagnosis lead, measured, not the dispatcher:** USA's `WA_AI_construction_timer` flag is **ABSENT** at 1941.9 and 1942.1 with rubber +26.7 / +10.0, imports 106.7 / 92.0, coal +8 415 / +7 007 — the C.10 gate was open on the trigger's own terms and Fix 80's escape is not what is blocking. Suspects in order: (1) the sibling `num_of_available_civilian_factories > 1` gate at `WA_AI_misc_on_actions.txt:70`, which sits OUTSIDE the Fix 80 OR and reads ~0 once the vanilla queue saturates (the R24 finding); (2) `WA_AI_PC_has_project_civs_20`; (3) the `any_of_scopes` free-core-site precondition at `events/WA_AI_construction.txt:1040-1043` (now stricter via R49's `synthetic_refinery < 6` term). Diagnose before re-tuning — see `documentation/WA_SYNTHETIC_RUBBER_PREBUILD.md`.

### R49. Shared-slot refinery queues do not stall on a state that hit its per-building cap (Fix 81)

- **Opened 2026-08-15** from a Discord report (community modder, not a campaign): `WA_AI_queue_AR` keeps targeting the same top-scored state after that state has reached `aluminium_refinery`'s `state_max = 15`, because `free_building_slots = { building = aluminium_refinery size > 0 }` only reports the **shared** slot pool and never consults the building's own `level_cap.state_max`. Code reading confirmed the mechanism (`WA_AI_CONSTRUCTION_scoring.txt` array is filtered by `WA_AI_available_SHARED` only; the queue loop breaks on the first state passing `WA_AI_available_AR`, which had no level term; `add_building_construction` past the cap silently no-ops). Same pattern for SR/HSR/HAR (cap 15) and REF (cap **6**, the easiest to saturate).
- **Fix under test:** Fix 81, in two cuts. **Cut 1** `7c4de2b53` (2026-08-15): `WA_AI_available_REF/SR/HSR/AR/HAR` in `common/scripted_triggers/WA_AI_CONSTRUCTION_triggers.txt` require `<building> < @WA_AI_<X>_STATE_MAX` (file-scoped constants that MUST match `common/buildings/00_buildings.txt`); also `WA_AI_queue_HAR` gated on `WA_AI_available_REF` (copy-paste) → `_HAR`. **The reporter rejected cut 1 as inadequate and was right:** the built level only rises when a refinery *finishes* (months), so a state stays below the cap on built level while its queue is already full and every pulse in between keeps feeding it — the stall was shortened to "until the batch completes", not removed. **Cut 2** (`55dc64cd7`, 2026-08-15) makes the check `built + queued < cap`: `WA_AI_add_<X>` (building_adders) increments the state variable `WA_AI_queued_<X>` (only when the add can land) and stamps `WA_AI_queued_<X>_recent` (TTL `@WA_AI_QUEUED_REFINERY_TTL_DAYS = 1000`); `WA_AI_reconcile_queued_refineries` (queue_functions, called per state from `WA_AI_get_shared_slot_scores` and from each adder) subtracts the rise in `building_level@<X>` since `WA_AI_last_level_<X>` and zeroes the counter once the TTL flag lapses (the only cancellation path script can see). Both variables exist only while something is queued. **Cut 2 was verified locally (see history) and found correct but cadence-bound:** the reconcile ran only at C.10 pulses, so GER state 51 read `built 6 + queued 1 = 7 > 6` for months after its line finished (GER's lane sat behind C.41's 1100-day `WA_AI_construction_timer`, so no pulse at all) — consumer-invisible for the queue loops (reconcile precedes the reads in the same pulse) but wrong in the save and blocking for R48's `any_of_scopes` precondition. **Cut 3** (`68ce50c6b`, 2026-08-15) stores the **sum**: `WA_AI_committed_<X>` = built + queued, which a completion leaves unchanged (one level moves from queued to built), so there is nothing to reconcile on any cadence. `WA_AI_available_<X>` = `building_level@X < cap` AND (`WA_AI_committed_<X>_recent` flag lapsed OR `committed < cap`); `WA_AI_add_<X>` rebases `committed` to the built level (unconditionally if the flag lapsed, upward clamp only while live), then `+1`, then stamps the flag (same TTL); `on_state_control_changed` clears the five flags. `WA_AI_reconcile_queued_refineries`, the `last_level` variables and the `get_shared_slot_scores` hook are gone. This is the reporter's proposal verbatim ("how many are in the state + how many have been queued").
- **Precondition (this item is only scoreable when it holds):** some AI country owns a core state whose `aluminium_refinery`, `steel_refinery`, `hydro_*_refinery` reached **15**, or `synthetic_refinery` reached **6**, at some save. If no country in the campaign ever caps a state, mark **NOT CHECKED** — a big shared-slot state plus a sustained resource deficit is needed to get there. Look first at the USA, GER, SOV and ENG late-war (1943+) `synthetic_refinery` counts: the REF cap of 6 is the most likely one to be hit.
- **Pass, two legs:**
  1. **No-stall leg (the fix itself).** For each (country, building) pair that satisfies the precondition at save *S*, the country's **total** level of that building across all its states is strictly higher at some later save *S+k* than at *S* — i.e. the building type kept being built **in other states** after the top state capped. A country whose per-type total freezes at exactly the value reached when its first state capped, while its deficit in the matching resource persists (`savegame.py resources <TAG>` EFFECTIVE column still negative), is a FAIL.
  2. **No-overshoot leg (the residual bound).** No state ever shows a level **above** the cap (16+ AR/SR/HSR/HAR, 7+ REF). Levels above the cap cannot come from this code path — the engine refuses them — so a value above cap is a data-reading error, not a Fix 81 fact; report it as an anomaly and re-check the parse.
  3. **Queue-phase leg (cut 3 — the part legs 1–2 cannot see).** Legs 1–2 only test the *built* cap; the queue-phase stall shows up on the state's `wa_ai_committed_<x>` variable. On every state carrying it with a live `WA_AI_committed_<X>_recent` flag: (a) `building_level ≤ wa_ai_committed_<x> ≤ cap` — above the cap means the gate is not counting, below the built level means an add path bypassed the adder; (b) `wa_ai_committed_<x> − building_level ≤` the summed `amount` of the owner's live lines for that building in that state (`production/general_lines`) — equality when the adder lines are the only ones, `<` when the engine's own construction AI merged levels into the same line (it does for CIC/MIC/NIC routinely, and it does not tell the sum); the sum stays constant across a completion (built +1, line gone, committed unchanged); (c) while some state of that country holds `committed = cap` for that type **and** the country's deficit is open, that type keeps appearing — new `wa_ai_committed_<x>` on other states, or a rising per-type total — i.e. the queue moved on. A `committed` that stays put after its flag lapsed is not a defect (the trigger ignores it then).
- **Probe:** `savegame.py buildings <TAG> <saves> --match refinery` for USA/GER/SOV/ENG/JAP/ITA on saves from 1941 onward, sampled every ~6 months, listing per-state levels; identify any state at cap and then track the country-wide total for that building type on the following saves. Pair with `savegame.py resources <TAG> <saves>` (EFFECTIVE column) to establish that the deficit was still open — a total that stops rising because the deficit closed is not a stall. For leg 3, grep the **state** blocks for `wa_ai_committed_` (state-scoped variable, not country) plus the state's `flags`, read `synthetic_refinery=`/`aluminium_refinery=` etc. from the same state block, and join against `production/general_lines` (the scratch extractors `refq.py` / `lines.py` / `report.py` from the 2026-08-15 local runs do exactly this, modulo the variable rename). **Absence contract:** if the campaign's build predates `7c4de2b53`, the item is NOT YET TESTED, never FAILED; if it predates `68ce50c6b`, leg 3 is NOT YET TESTED and legs 1–2 score as before (a leg 1–2 pass on an earlier cut does not clear the item — the streak needs the cut-3 build).
- **Threshold:** 3 (narrow — the pass is a discrete "did the per-type total keep rising after the first capped state / does the counter invariant hold" fact off one sweep). **Since Fix 88 v2 (`fe6d95612`)** the sum also exists for CIC/MIC/NIC (`wa_ai_committed_cic/mic/nic`, cap 45) — leg 3 applies to them too, and the two forgetting paths are now counted: `wa_tlm_sq_rebase_down_n` (TTL lapsed, sum above built) and `wa_tlm_sq_ctrl_reset_n` (live flag dropped on control change) — read them with `savegame.py tlm <TAG> <save> --match sq`; a campaign with many state losses and `ctrl_reset_n = 0` on the losers means the on_action reset is not firing. Streak restarted at 0 with cut 3: the cut-1 pass on `af003548` and the cut-2 local runs are kept in the history as evidence but do not count.
- **Deliberately NOT done, and why:**
  - **No completion hook, and no reconcile.** HOI4 has no on_action for "building finished" or "construction cancelled". Cut 3 needs neither because the tracked quantity (built + queued) does not change on completion; the TTL flag remains for the cancellation case only. A shorter TTL re-admits a state whose old line still exists (the engine refuses adds past the real cap, so at most `cap − built` refused adds on that one state before the sum climbs back — bounded, one state, only after 1000 days without an add); a longer one only delays a cancelled state's release. 1000 days is deliberately long — lines have been seen unfunded for 12+ months.
  - **No WA_TLM probe.** The fingerprint is a plain building level already in the save; a counter incremented on the `< cap` term would fire on code-path entry, which the telemetry standard forbids.
- **Regression watch:**
  - **Constants drift.** If a future balance pass changes a `state_max` in `00_buildings.txt` without touching the `@WA_AI_*_STATE_MAX` constants, the AI either stalls again (constant too high) or leaves slots on the table (constant too low). Any campaign showing a country-wide per-type total frozen below what its capped states allow should check the two files agree first.
  - **R48's `any_of_scopes` precondition** on the proactive REF pre-build now carries the `synthetic_refinery < 6` term via `WA_AI_available_REF`. Stricter, not looser — but if R48 leg 1 shows `_n = 0` on a country whose only free-slot cores are all at 6 REF, that is this term, not the R48 trigger.
- **Streak:** 0
- **Status:** NOT YET TESTED (cut 3)
- **History:**
  - NOT YET TESTED — fix postdates every campaign in the registry.
  - 2026-08-16 · local fork of `af003548` at 1940.6.1, run 12 months on cut 3 (`68ce50c6b`), save `BHU_1941_06_14_15.hoi4` (1941.6.14) — leg 3 **PASSED** locally (local runs are evidence, not streak): build fingerprint clean (0 `wa_ai_queued_*` / `wa_ai_last_level_*`; 9 states carry `wa_ai_committed_*` + flag, no var/flag mismatch). **Completion case that broke cut 2 now correct:** GER 51 REF built 5→6, adder line finished, `committed 6 = built 6 + 0 lines`, cap 6, no over-count. 8 of 9 rows exact `committed − built == Σ amount` of live adder lines (SOV 217/219/249/651/1069 REF, JAP 282 REF, ENG 123/131 AR where C.63's five `WA_AI_queue_AR` calls split 4+1 exactly); SOV 249 exercised the rebase path (2 levels built outside the adder, add rebased to 2 then +1 = 3). One `committed < built` (GER 51 SR 4 vs 5): a level arrived through the **engine's own construction AI** — `refinery = yes` buildings are queued by the vanilla construction AI too, and those lines/completions are invisible to the sum (the four counter-less post-fork lines SWI 3 / HUN 889 / MEX 480 / USA 834 are engine-AI, **not** focus/decision as the cut-2 note said). Under-count only ever over-admits by the engine's own levels and the next add rebases upward — not a gate defect. No level > cap, no committed > cap. No-stall: SOV/JAP/ENG loops keep placing on uncapped states (5 synth + 2 AR adder lines vs 0 on the cloud branch); GER cannot show "moved on" — C.10 fired once (1940.7.10) then C.41 locked its lane 1100 d from 1941.1.2. Standing side-finding: 8 of 9 adder lines still unfunded 1–12 months later.
  - 2026-08-16 · local fork of `af003548` at 1940.6.1, run 17 months on Fix 88 v2 (`fe6d95612`, all eight types), save `BHU_1941_11_18_12.hoi4` (1941.11.18) — leg 3 **PASSED** locally on 8 types: 164 (state,type) cells, **0 over-count**, 0 level > cap, 0 committed > cap; all REF/AR cells exact; the 56 non-exact CIC/MIC/NIC cells are all `committed < built + Σ lines` — engine-AI completions (23), engine merges into a pre-fork line (18) or into the adder's own line (8), and 7 flag-cleared states after control change (SOV → GER/ROM) whose stale sum is unused by design. `wa_tlm_sq_rebase_down_n = 0` everywhere (no TTL lapse yet, expected). **Bug found:** `wa_tlm_sq_ctrl_reset_n` was written as a STATE variable (7 states = 1, every country 0) — inside the `FROM.FROM` block `FROM` re-binds to the state; fixed by counting from the effect root (`7f7004c66`).
  - 2026-08-15 · cut 3 (`68ce50c6b`) supersedes cut 2 — streak reset to 0. Cut-2 local verification (campaign `af003548` forked at 1940.6.1, saves `GER_1940_09_18_06`, `autosave` 1941.6.1, `GER_1941_06_10_01`, build `55dc64cd7`): gate WORKS — 6 synth lines / 8 levels queued by GER/SOV/JAP on non-capped states vs **0** synth lines on the pre-fix cloud branch over the same window; every counter matched exactly one engine line (SOV 651: two adds merged into one `amount=2` line, counter 2); ENG 131 `wa_ai_queued_ar = 5` = line `amount=5`; no level above cap; no uncounted line from the shared-slot loops (the only counter-less lines come from focus/decision files). Defect found: GER 51 finished 5→6 but kept `queued 1 / last_level 5` (7 > 6) — reconcile only at C.10 pulses and GER's lane sat behind C.41's 1100-day timer → cut 3. Side findings kept here: 5 of the 6 AI-queued refinery lines were still **unfunded 10–12 months later** (engine allocation, separate matter); C.41 silences GER's standard construction lane 1941.1→~1944.1 (pre-existing, confirm intent).
  - 2026-08-15 · cut 2 (`55dc64cd7`) superseded cut 1 — the `af003548` line below stays as legs 1–2 evidence only.
  - 2026-08-15 · `af003548` · PASSED on cut-1 build (legs 1–2 only, does not count toward the streak) — Precondition met on three countries: GER state 799 `synthetic_refinery` = **6 (cap)** from 1941.6 while GER synth total grows 36 → 41 elsewhere; USA state 825 `steel_refinery` = **15 (cap)** from 1941.6, USA steel 178 → 192; SOV state 563 synth 6 at 1945.6, SOV synth 35 → 60 with 408/409 also reaching 6; ENG synth 4 → 21. Leg 1 PASS — every capped country's per-type total keeps rising in other states. Leg 2 PASS — full census 6 saves × 6 countries × 5 refinery types finds **ZERO** states above cap. Constants match `00_buildings.txt`. USA HAR frozen at 15 (state 368) is "no queue" (HAR is not queued by anything), not a stall. Streak 1 of 3.

### R50. The Narvik landing of Weserübung holds (Fix 82)

- **Opened 2026-08-15** from a player report ("the scripted German invasion of Norway fails on the Narvik landing every time"). Code reading of `events/WA_AI_invasions.txt` (`WA_AI_invasions.3`, fired 1940.4.9 by `WA_KDE_yearly_event_fire_1940`): the Nord-Norge (state 144) wave was the only Weserübung wave with **one province and 2 divisions** (Vestlandet 3×2, Trøndelag 2×2, Østlandet 3×2 motorised), dropped on province 192 — the very province where NOR's *6. Divisjon* starts (`history/units/NOR_1936.txt`) — in a 19-province mountain state with no land link to the southern beachheads. The displaced 6th Division plus the Finnmark brigade retook the port.
- **Fix under test:** Fix 82. Nord-Norge wave is now a contiguous three-province pocket — 192 Narvik, 3034 (coastal, west) and 13878 (inland); all three are mutually adjacent per `WA_AI_MAP_province_connections` — **2 divisions on Narvik, 1 on each flank** (4 total, 20-width infantry, template 7; two spawn calls each with its own dpp assignment + ADJUST, dpp reset to 2 afterwards) at `_start_experience = 0.7` instead of the 0.45 default, reset right after the wave. Weserübung's designed national delta moves from **22 to 24** on normal (44 → 48 on hard); the R18 retired note's "22 designed" is the pre-Fix-82 figure. Also stripped a pre-existing UTF-8 BOM from `events/WA_AI_invasions.txt` (was loading anyway — the events parser is BOM-tolerant — but rule 16 forbids it).
- **Precondition:** GER is AI, historical difficulty, at war with NOR on 1940.4.9 with NOR controlling 1018 and GER/ally controlling Jutland (99) — the existing `.3` gate. If GER never fights NOR in 1940, NOT CHECKED.
- **Pass, two legs:**
  1. **Delivery leg.** At the 1940.5.1 save, GER's Norway division delta over April is ≥ 24 (per-state counts are a floor; the national delta is the validated metric — see R18), and provinces 192, 3034, 13878 are GER-controlled at 1940.5.1.
  2. **Hold leg (the fix itself).** Province 192 (Narvik) is controlled by GER or a GER ally at **each** of the 1940.6.1, 1940.7.1 and 1940.8.1 saves, or NOR has capitulated by 1940.8.1. A 192 that flips back to NOR/ENG/FRA at any of those saves while NOR is still fighting is a FAIL.
- **Probe:** `savegame.py` province-controller read for 192/3034/13878 on the 1940.4/5/6/7/8 saves; GER unit count in states 144/142/143/110/1017/1020 at 1940.4.1 vs 1940.5.1 for the delta. **Absence contract:** builds predating Fix 82 are NOT YET TESTED, never FAILED.
- **Threshold:** 3 (a discrete province-control fact per campaign).
- **Deliberately NOT done, and why:**
  - **No fourth province (13879), no 2-per-flank.** Nord-Norge is unsupplied except through Narvik's port; 4 divisions on normal / 8 on hard is the ceiling the port can plausibly feed. Wider or deeper stacks would just bleed org.
  - **No change to `history/units/GER_Norway.txt`** (5th/6th Gebirgsjäger placed at 192/3034 by `den_armor.6`/`ger_armor.928`) — separate mechanism, untouched.
- **Regression watch:** if a campaign shows GER's Norway delta at 1940.5.1 near 22 rather than 24 (or 44 rather than 48 on hard) the extra provinces did not spawn — check the `create_unit` meta_effect log for 3034/13878.
- **Streak:** 1
- **Status:** PASSED
- **History:**
  - NOT YET TESTED — fix postdates every campaign in the registry.
  - 2026-08-15 · `af003548` · PASSED (first test) — Delivery leg: GER 0 divisions in Norway at 1940.4.1 → **26** across the 10 NOR states at 1940.5.1 (≥ 24 — both added provinces spawned); provinces 192/3034/13878 GER-controlled at 1940.5.1; exactly **4** GER divisions in state 144 (the 2+1+1 pocket). Hold leg: province 192 explicit `controller="GER"` at 1940.6.1 / 1940.7.1 / 1940.8.1 with NOR uncapitulated and fielding 5–6 divisions; the Allied counter-landing (ENG 5 + FRA 3 divisions at 1940.5.1) beaten; all NOR states GER by 1940.9.1. Streak 1 of 3.

### R51. A landing that is still ahead on the ground keeps its execute orders — front-local hysteresis (Fix 83)

- **Opened 2026-08-15** from campaign `af003548`. D-Day fired 1944.6.7, Paris fell 1944.6.22, and at 1944.8 the Allies held 6 of 24 metropolitan French states with **121 divisions against 95 German**. One month later they held 2 (the beaches, 15 + 1016) with 82 against 142, and stayed on those two states for 20 of the next 22 months. Both ENG and USA read `wa_ai_military_posture_vs_ger` **1 (1944.7) → 0 (1944.8) → 0 (1944.9) → 1 (1944.10)** with `wa_ai_fielded_eq_ratio` never below 0.930 (not the Fix 55 cliff), the pairwise `fighting_army_strength_ratio` hopeless as always for a coalition member, and the global air veto silent (GER 14 109 × 0.4 = 5 644 against ENG ~8 900). The binding term is the level-1 **front-local branch** — `_post_local_friendly > 1.5 × _post_local_enemy` in `common/scripted_effects/WA_AI_MILITARY_posture_effects.txt` — a single bar for entering AND holding, so 121/95 = 1.27 retracted every `front_control … execute_order = yes` the western Allies held against Germany the month Germany reinforced Normandy 26 → 95 → 142 divisions off a quiet Eastern front (GER-held SOV states 5 → 0 by 1944.12, GER army flat at 285–291). The unordered beachhead was rolled up. Air was a real but secondary factor (GER 1.22:1 over region 239 at 1944.8) and belongs to R15.
- **Fix under test:** Fix 83 — `@WA_AI_POSTURE_LOCAL_RATIO_HOLD = 1.1`: a country already at posture > 0 against this enemy keeps level 1 while its side's local count still exceeds 1.1 × the enemy's; entering still costs the full 1.5. Same enter/hold shape as Fix 55's `MIN_EQ` / `MIN_EQ_HOLD`. At 1944.9's 82 vs 142 (0.58) the hold band releases too — the fix keeps orders on a beachhead that is still ahead, not on one already overturned. Probe family `WA_TLM_r51_local_hold_n / _first_t / _last_t` (v16) counts level-1 verdicts that ONLY the hold band delivered.
- **Precondition:** an AI western landing in Europe against an uncapitulated Axis major, with the landing side's local count falling under 1.5× within 3 months of the landing (the `af003548` shape). If the local count never drops under 1.5 (or the landing is not contested), the mechanism leg is NOT CHECKED — score the outcome leg only.
- **Pass, two legs:**
  1. **Mechanism leg.** `wa_tlm_r51_local_hold_n > 0` on at least one western major with `_first_t` decoded inside [landing month, landing month + 3], AND that major's `wa_ai_military_posture_vs_<enemy>` does **not** read 0 in any monthly save of that window while its `wa_ai_fielded_eq_ratio` ≥ 0.90 and its side's divisions in the theatre exceed the enemy's. A posture 0 in the window with the count still ahead = the band did not hold = FAILED.
  2. **Outcome leg.** The Allied-controlled metropolitan French state count does not fall by ≥ 50% in any single month of the 6 months after the landing while Allied divisions in France exceed Axis divisions in France (`af003548`: 6 → 2 at 1944.9 with 82 < 142 — that particular month would be excused by the count, but 1944.8's 121 > 95 preceded it; the leg tests the month of the collapse, not the month before).
- **Probe:** `savegame.py tlm ENG <last-save> --match "^wa_tlm_r51_"` and the same for USA (decode `_first_t`); `savegame.py var ENG "^wa_ai_military_posture_vs_|^wa_ai_fielded_eq_ratio=" <monthly saves landing→+6>` and USA likewise; divisions-in-France by side per month via the `units`-scoped state walk used for R34/F5 (`units` only, cross-checked against `savegame.py army`); Allied-controlled French states per month from `states={}` with `controller=` defaulting to owner. **Absence contract:** `wa_tlm_version < 16` = NOT YET TESTED, never FAILED.
- **Threshold:** 5 (behavioural — whether a contested landing survives its first counter-attack is an outcome).
- **Regression watch:** (a) a country holding execute orders at posture 1 into a front where the local count has fallen below 1.0 for 2+ months (`_n` climbing, `posture_vs` 1, divisions in theatre < enemy's) — the band should have released; (b) `_n` large on SOV vs GER or JAP vs CHI (fronts where the entry bar was already met) — the band should be doing nothing there; (c) any change to `WA_AI_MILITARY_posture_vs_*` behaviour on the level-2 grind, which Fix 83 does not touch.
- **Deliberately NOT done, and why:** no hysteresis on the pairwise `EXEC_RATIO` (0.85) — no campaign has shown it flapping, and it is the quality-weighted channel; and `LOCAL_RATIO` itself is not lowered — 1.5 was set so a marginal count does not start an offensive.
- **Not addressed by this item (recorded so it is not rediscovered):** the **18-month freeze** that followed the rollback (1944.9 → 1946.3: posture back at 1, 71–115 Allied vs 57–97 Axis divisions, two state flips) is a different question — the front had orders and did not convert. Fix 83 explains the rollback, not the freeze; the freeze needs its own diagnosis (supply/org of 70–115 divisions on two states, GER 1.2–1.5:1 air over the pocket, USA 92–94% of aircraft without mission).
- **Absorbed from R37 (posture equipment-gate hysteresis, Fix 55) on 2026-08-16.** Fix 55 (`@WA_AI_POSTURE_MIN_EQ_HOLD = 0.85` beside `@WA_AI_POSTURE_MIN_EQ = 0.9`, `WA_AI_MILITARY_posture_effects.txt`, renumbered from Fix 53) is the *equipment-gate* half of the same enter/hold shape Fix 83 gives the local-ratio branch; its root cause was the `02bd4445` cliff (ENG eq 0.947 → 0.887 in one month, `posture_vs_ger` 2 → 0, every western execute order retracted, 13 French states flipped without a battle, reversed when ENG recovered to 0.915). R37 was scored three times (`f9321934`, `be18f9c7`, `af003548`) and its hysteresis leg was **NOT CHECKED every time** — no major ever entered the [0.85, 0.90] band while holding posture > 0 (minima ENG 0.9125 / 0.9733 / 0.927, USA 0.9423 / 0.9315 / 0.945, GER 0.9331 / 0.905→0.777 skipping the band with posture already 0, SOV 0.9707 / 0.917); its outcome leg (no month with ≥ 8 French flips while both sides move < 15%) PASSED all three. **Legs kept alive here as R51 leg 3 (equipment-hold, same rule):** if any major's `wa_ai_fielded_eq_ratio` dips into [0.85, 0.90] while `wa_ai_military_posture_vs_<enemy> > 0`, posture does not fall to 0 that month — sample **monthly** (`savegame.py var <TAG> "^wa_ai_fielded_eq_ratio=" …`, trailing `=` anchor); band never entered = NOT CHECKED. Two findings R37 recorded that still stand: grind mode (`WA_TLM_post_grind_n`) is near-dead code (20/9 vs ~4 500 level-1 observations, 51/37/30/0 on `be18f9c7`); and the `be18f9c7` "USA posture 2 → 0 at 1944.9 with eq 0.9315" oddity is the local-ratio term this item fixes.
- **Streak:** 0
- **Status:** NOT YET TESTED
- **History:**
  - 2026-08-16 · `af003548` **local 10-day-class probe** (`USA_1944_10_01_11.hoi4`, resumed from cloud `1944.7_Jul` on the v16 build, single save 1944.10.1) · **MOVED — not scored (one save, no monthly posture series).** Mechanism: `wa_tlm_r51_local_hold_n = 3` on BOTH ENG and USA, `_first_t` 1944.7, `_last_t` 1944.8 — the hold band bit in exactly the diagnosed window; posture_vs_ger 1 on both at 1944.10 with eq 0.940 / 0.9345. Outcome: **4 Allied-held metropolitan French states at 1944.10 (15, 1016, 785 Picardy, 855 Paris) vs 2 on the cloud path**, with Germany STRONGER in the branch (90 Axis divisions in France vs 67 cloud; GER army 288 vs 277) and Allied 84 divisions ashore. Not a lighter opponent. Streak untouched; first cloud campaign scores it.
  - NOT YET TESTED — fix postdates every campaign in the registry (`af003548` is the diagnosing campaign).

### R52. The theatre air-base builder builds where the builder's own army is fighting (Fix 85)

- **Opened 2026-08-15** from campaign `af003548`. After D-Day the Western Europe air theatre was **contested and in capacity deficit for USA and ENG at every save 1944.7 → 1945.9** (allied-held capacity 21 levels vs a 26–36 level target) and `WA_AI_build_theatre_air_bases` ran every pulse (`wa_ai_thair_dbg_called` +4–5/month, `dbg_active` 4–6) — yet `dbg_started` **froze for 8 months on both majors** (USA 38 from 1944.8, ENG 25 from 1944.7). USA never queued an air base in France at all (its `theatre_air` picks were 163 Balkans, 1002 New Guinea, 1033 Indochina); ENG queued two (24, 785, 1944.7, both level 0), both starved at 0 factories for five months behind level-8→12 `uk_air` projects in the same 300 band, then were purged. Capacity on Allied-controlled French soil: **700 planes** (15 at level 4, 1016 at level 3); ENG's 1 200 planes "in France" at 1944.11 sat 4× overstacked on 1016. The USAAF kept 6 000–7 800 land-based planes in the continental US, 92–94% without a mission — while the UK looked 30–45 % empty when counting planes present (capacity 10 700 → 13 000 vs 5 900–8 000 planes in the 11 hosting states). **CORRECTED 2026-08-16:** that emptiness was over-read. Re-measured on `1944.7_Jul` over ALL tags: the 11 hosting states hold 8 196 planes in 79 wings against 10 600 capacity (77 % actual, **90 % if every wing books at least 100 slots**), the whole UK 9 617/13 600 (71 % / 81 %); the save stores only `count=` (planes present, = Σ `equipment.amount` on 1 117/1 117 wings), but the nominal wing size IS derivable: the wing pool's `definition=` is the `sub_units` key of `common/units/air.txt`, whose `land_air_wing_size` is 100 for every type except tac_bomber 200, strat/heavy_strat 300, scout/maritime_patrol 25 — and on THAT measure **7 of the 11 hosting states sit at exactly 100.0 % (Kent 1 200/1 200, Sussex 1 000/1 000, N. London 900/900, Dorset/Gloucs/Leics/Warwick 800/800)** while their plane counts read 86–98 % — the signature of the engine booking capacity per wing size, exactly as the modder observed in game. Aggregate 9 550/10 600 = 90 % nominal in the hosting states (the four misses are under-populated fields: Essex 75 % with two 25-plane scout wings, Norfolk 75 %, Cornwall 56 %, S. London 92 %), UK total 11 150/13 600 = 82 %; the USAAF at home is likewise packed — 6 600 nominal on the 18 CONUS states it uses at 94 %, with 6 600 CONUS capacity standing empty on 19 unused fields. Boston at 900/800 shows the engine tolerates overstuffing (CAPACITY_PENALTY) rather than refusing. By this rule the hosting states are full and the UK's structural ceiling (12 levels × 11 states = 13 200) cannot host RAF ~8 000 + USAAF ~9 000 at any measure. The USAAF's binder IS capacity in range; the continental capacity Fix 85 builds is the only lever that scales. Two causes inside the builder: (a) `random_state` draws uniformly across every deficit theatre's states from ladder rung 0, so a landing theatre whose only allied states are already at level 3–4 loses to Pacific/Balkan level-0 islands every pulse; (b) `theatre_air` shares the 300 band with `uk_air` and, sorted behind it, never gets a factory.
- **Fix under test:** Fix 85 (`common/scripted_effects/WA_AI_CONSTRUCTION_PRIORITY_strategies.txt`, `WA_AI_build_theatre_air_bases`). A **ground-committed** theatre = a deficit theatre with ≥ 1 ROOT division standing on a side-controlled member state (detected inside the existing pass-2 state walk, no new scan, no theatre list, no tag). While any committed theatre is in deficit the ladder draws only from committed theatres; the per-state cap there is `@THEATRE_AIR_TARGET_LEVEL_COMMITTED = 8` (vs 4); priority `@AI_PC_PRIO_AIR_FRONT = 350` (new band "air-front", above air-basing 300, below rail-prewar 500) so it leads the Fix 78 lane and the sorted fill. Countries with no divisions in any deficit theatre keep the uniform spread. Probes: `WA_TLM_r52_thair_front_n / _first_t / _last_t` (v17 build) at the verified start; snapshot `wa_ai_thair_dbg_committed`.
- **Precondition:** an AI major with > 99 deployed planes lands divisions in a contested air theatre whose allied-held capacity is below the builder's target. If no such landing occurs, NOT CHECKED.
- **Pass, three legs:**
  1. **Selection leg.** `wa_tlm_r52_thair_front_n > 0` on the landing power(s) with `_first_t` decoded within 2 months of the landing, and `pc <TAG> --match air_base` on the following saves shows ≥ 1 `theatre_air` project whose target state is IN the landing theatre (for a Normandy landing: one of 15/1016/24/785/855/16/29…), not Balkans/Pacific.
  2. **Funding leg.** That project reaches `assigned_factories > 0` within 8 weeks of being queued (`pc` stall column ≤ 8 at the first save after queueing, or the level in the target state rises on the next save).
  3. **Outcome leg.** Air-base levels on Allied-controlled states of the landing theatre rise by ≥ 4 levels total within 6 months of the landing (`af003548` baseline: +1 in 12 months — 1016 went 2 → 3), AND the landing power's land-based planes located in the theatre exceed 500 at some save within 6 months (`af003548`: USA 0 at every sampled date).
- **Probe:** `savegame.py tlm USA <saves> --match "^wa_tlm_r52_"` and same for ENG; `savegame.py var USA "^wa_ai_thair_dbg_" <saves>`; `savegame.py pc USA <saves> --match air_base --limit 0` and same for ENG; air-base levels per state from `states={}` (`controller=` defaults to owner); USA/ENG land-based planes by base state via `strategic_air → TAG → air_wing_pool → air_wings` + the depth-1 `air_base={}` map (wings whose base has no `state=` are carrier decks — exclude). Room on the landing-theatre fields: `airload.py --states 15,1016,24,785,855,16,29 <saves>` — read NOMINAL vs cap, not planes present. **Absence contract:** `wa_tlm_version < 17` = NOT YET TESTED.
- **Threshold:** 3 (narrow — the selection and funding legs are discrete facts off two sweeps; the outcome leg is a level count).
- **Regression watch:** (a) `r52_thair_front_n` climbing on a country whose only committed theatre is where its CAPITAL sits (home defence is the engine's job — the pull blocks exclude it, the builder now should not be spending there either; if seen, add the capital-theatre exclusion to the committed test); (b) `uk_air` projects on ENG/USA back at 0 factories for 3+ months while a 350 project is funded — the lane is one slot, three 350 projects at 1 700–2 900 IC each should cycle in weeks, not months; (c) the Pacific island chain (the case this builder was written for) no longer getting starts for the USA while USA divisions sit in France — that is by design while WEU is in deficit, but if the Pacific theatre stays in deficit for 12+ months with the USA committed there too and `_n` only ever landing in France, the random draw within committed theatres is not spreading.
- **NOT addressed here (recorded so it is not rediscovered):** the UK ceiling itself. Corrected 2026-08-16 (see the opening paragraph): the "3 800–5 700 spare slots" reading counted planes present; on a per-wing-100 booking the hosting states are ~90 % full and the UK's 13 200-slot structural ceiling cannot take RAF + USAAF together, so the USAAF's binder is capacity in range after all. Two levers remain outside this item: (a) widen the UK hosting-state list beyond the 11 southern states for bomber/transport wings that do not need Channel range (Wales 400/400, Lancashire 300/300 already full; N. Ireland / Highlands / Lanark at 0 planes on 500 capacity — small), (b) the France capacity this fix builds — 2 states × 8 levels = 1 600 slots — is the real ceiling until more of France is liberated. The theatre pull itself and the engine's willingness to rebase were NOT shown defective.
- **Streak:** 0
- **Status:** NOT YET TESTED
- **History:**
  - 2026-08-16 · `af003548` **local probe** (`USA_1944_10_01_11.hoi4`, resumed from cloud `1944.7_Jul` on v16, single save 1944.10.1) · **selection + funding MOVED, outcome leg (aircraft) DID NOT — not scored.** `wa_tlm_r52_thair_front_n` USA **8** (`_first_t` 1944.7, `_last_t` 1944.9), ENG 1 (1944.9); `wa_ai_thair_dbg_committed = 1` both; `dbg_started` USA 38 → **45**, ENG 25 → 26. USA queue: `theatre_air` on **15 Lower Normandy and 855 Paris at band air-front 350, 20 factories each, stall 0** (cloud: zero theatre_air, three uk_air), plus 117 Campania @350 at 0 factories stall 6. Air-base levels on Allied-held French soil **+4 in 4 months** (1016 3 → 5, 855 2 → 4; bar ≥ 4 in 6 months met). BUT USA land-based planes in France still **0**, USA no-mission share 79 → **87 %**, ENG 31 → 39 %, GER:Allied over region 239 **1.63** (cloud 1.33) — the bases were built and the aircraft did not follow (the residual R52 already records, R15's). Two watch items confirmed live: ENG's single 350 slot went to **1032 Southern Sicily** (ENG is committed in Italy too — the draw is uniform among committed theatres) while 785 Picardy sat at 300/0 factories 4 months; and USA's third 350 project (Campania) starved behind the other two (regression watch (b), mirror image). Streak untouched.
  - NOT YET TESTED — fix postdates every campaign in the registry (`af003548` is the diagnosing campaign).

### R53. Standard construction bursts spread across the top-K states instead of one deep line (Fix 88)

- **Opened 2026-08-16** from the Fix 81 reporter, after cut 3 was confirmed working: "do we want it to queue so deeply? US has ~20 parallel construction lines in 1940, I queued 20 alu mills and it occupies 2 or 3 lines". Cause: every shared-slot `WA_AI_queue_<X>` broke on the first eligible state of the score-sorted array, and the engine merges same-(state, building) adds into ONE line (`amount = N`) that takes ≤ 20 civs (WA `MAX_CIV_FACTORIES_PER_LINE`) and builds serially. Cut-3 saves show it plainly: ENG C.63's five `WA_AI_queue_AR` calls → 131 `amount=4` + 123 `amount=1`.
- **Fix under test:** Fix 88, commits `340294482` + `870ae45c4` (3-pass version) superseded the same day by **v2 `fe6d95612`**: `WA_AI_queue_CIC/CIC_2/MIC/MIC_2/NIC/REF/HSR/SR/HAR/AR` (`WA_AI_CONSTRUCTION_queue_functions.txt`) make ONE selection walk over the score-sorted array and classify every eligible state into four tiers (`WA_AI_bf_consider` / `WA_AI_bf_choose`): **A** rank < K, under the per-line depth cap, not yet picked this pulse; **B** rank < K, under depth, already picked → wrap around (picked set cleared, round-robin restarts); **C** rank < K, over depth, not picked — depth is a *preference*, never a wall; **D** first eligible in score order = exactly the pre-Fix-88 pick (picked set cleared) — so no country builds less than before. `K = ceil((num_of_civilian_factories − WA_AI_PC_assigned_factories_total) / 20)` clamped `[1, 20]` (20 = WA `MAX_CIV_FACTORIES_PER_LINE`; `WA_AI_queue_breadth_prepare`). **All eight shared-slot types** now carry the Fix 81 sum (`WA_AI_available_CIC/MIC/NIC` added: built < 45 AND (TTL lapsed OR committed < 45); adders maintain `WA_AI_committed_CIC/MIC/NIC`; control-change reset covers all eight flags) and a depth trigger `WA_AI_queue_depth_ok_<X>` (`@WA_AI_QUEUE_DEPTH_<X> = 3`). Telemetry: standing family **`WA_TLM_sq_*`** (v17): `adds_n`, `adds_by_type^0..7` (CIC MIC NIC REF SR HSR AR HAR), `first_t/last_t`, `tier_a_n/_b_n/_c_n/_d_n`, `k` (monthly gauge), `k_max`, `rebase_down_n`, `ctrl_reset_n` — `documentation/WA_TLM_TELEMETRY_SYSTEM.md` §6d. Reviewed twice by `wa-lessons-reviewer` (3-pass version: own `_wa_bf_break_`, cadence timeline, C.72 walk; v2: see commit).
- **Pass, four legs:**
  1. **Spread leg (the fix itself).** For a country with K > 1, an event that issues N ≥ 2 `WA_AI_queue_<X>` calls the same day (C.10 batches: 13 CIC / 52 MIC / 24 NIC / 4 REF / 2 SR / 2 AR; C.63 5 AR + 35 MIC + 15 NIC; C.72 354 MIC; C.74/78/79 SR/AR) produces adder-made lines (`production/general_lines`, same `created_date`) on **min(N, K, #eligible)** distinct states, not 1–3. On the cut-3 local branch (pre-Fix-88) ENG C.63 = 2 AR states, USA C.72 = ≤ 8 MIC states; post-fix expect ENG ≥ 4 AR states (K = 4 at 61 civs: ceil(61/20)), USA up to 20 MIC states.
  2. **Depth leg.** No refinery line made by the adders carries `amount > 3` while other eligible states of that owner exist (`amount = 4` on ENG 131 was the pre-fix shape); `wa_ai_committed_<x> − building_level ≤ 3` on every state with a live flag, except when pass 3 had to fire (every candidate at depth — then the old shape is allowed and the header says so).
  3. **Tier/telemetry leg.** `savegame.py tlm <TAG> <save> --match sq` on the majors: `wa_tlm_sq_adds_n > 0` with `first_t/last_t` live; `tier_a_n + tier_b_n + tier_c_n + tier_d_n` = adds made through the queue functions (the remainder of `adds_n` = hard-coded state sites); **`tier_d_n / adds_n` small** (breadth exhausted only when nothing inside the top K is eligible — a large share means K or the eligibility is too tight for that country); `sq_k` consistent with `ceil((wa_tlm_pc_civs − pc_assigned)/20)`; `adds_by_type^i` non-zero for the types the country's events actually queue. Absence contract: `wa_tlm_version < 17` → this leg NOT CHECKED.
  4. **Not-less leg (the reviewer's demand).** Per (owner, building type), the number of adder-made lines and levels created over a 12-month window is **≥** the pre-fix branch over the same window (local `af003548` fork numbers at 1941.6: SOV 4 synth lines, JAP 1, ENG 2 AR / 5 levels, USA 0 refinery lines with C.72's MICs at 8 states); and total built CIC/MIC/refinery per owner at +12 months is not lower than the cut-3 branch.
- **Probe:** `lines.py` (scratchpad extractor from the 2026-08-15/16 local runs; join `production/general_lines` for the 8 templates by owner, state, `created_date`, `amount`) grouped by (owner, template, created_date) → distinct-state count per burst; `refq3.py` for `wa_ai_committed_*`; `savegame.py buildings <TAG> … --match "arms|industrial|refinery"` for totals; `savegame.py var <TAG> "^wa_tlm_r48_c10_fire_n"` to know how many C.10 firings the window had (K only matters when a burst fired). **Absence contract:** build predates `340294482` → NOT YET TESTED.
- **Threshold:** 3 (narrow — distinct-state counts per burst, TLM tier counters and per-window totals off one sweep).
- **Regression watch:**
  - **Civ absorption on bulk bursts.** C.72 now opens up to 20 MIC lines at once (~360 civs at 20/line) instead of 8. Watch USA 1940.7–1941.7: `wa_ai_pc_assigned_factories_total` must keep its floored share, and CIC/infra totals must not flatline while the MIC program runs. If they do, the fix is right and the burst event is over-sized — clamp K in that event, do not touch the loops.
  - **Lower-scored states inside top-K.** Breadth places into states 2..K of the score order; if a campaign shows CIC/MIC lines in states with `infrastructure ≤ 3` or under air attack, the top-K bound is too loose for that country class — lower `@WA_AI_BF_MAX_K` or add a score floor, again not the loops.
  - **`break` contract.** `queue_INF` fallback (`queue_MIC`/`queue_CIC` when nothing was added) and R48's save/restore read plain `break`; the new loops set it on every add. A campaign where INF lines appear on the same day as a successful CIC/MIC burst for the same country means that contract broke.
- **Streak:** 0
- **Status:** NOT YET TESTED
- **History:**
  - 2026-08-16 · local fork of `af003548` at 1940.6.1, run 17 months on v2 (`fe6d95612`), save `BHU_1941_11_18_12.hoi4` — **all four legs PASSED locally** (evidence, not streak): leg 1 spread — ENG C.63 (1941.6.6): 5 AR calls → **5 states** (cloud 2), 35 MIC → **13 new lines / 13 states** (cloud 2), 15 NIC → **15 states** (cloud 9); USA C.72 (1940.7.7, 354 MIC) → **33 states**, top amounts 21/14/14/12/11 (cloud: 25 states, 21/17/17/16/15 — the pre-fix baseline was already 25, not the header's "~8": `WA_AI_available_MIC`'s `free_building_slots` debits per queued level, so deep lines fill a state's slots and roll over; v2 widens and flattens); SOV C.10 1941.3.28: 14 CIC states; JAP 6 MIC states. Leg 2 depth — ENG/GER/JAP all lines ≤ 3 (`tier_c_n = 0`); USA MIC max 21 and SOV CIC max 4 exactly where `tier_c_n` (216 / 19) says depth was over-ridden by breadth. Leg 3 TLM — `wa_tlm_version = 17`, Σ`tier_a..d` = `adds_n` on every country (USA 372: 100/50/216/6, `d` share 1.6 %; SOV 144: 119/6/19/0; ENG 55: 53/2/0/0; GER 19: 19/0/0/0; JAP 9), no scope-encoded value, `k` in [1,20] (stored fractional — effective K = ceil, doc'd), `adds_by_type` matches each country's events. Leg 4 not-less — SOV 20 CIC lines / 3 REF vs cloud 0 / 0, USA MIC 39 lines vs 25, ENG MIC 13 / NIC 15 / AR 4 vs 2 / 9 / 2, JAP REF 1 vs 0; built totals ≥ cloud for GER/JAP/USA/ENG/RAJ (SOV MIC/NIC lower = Barbarossa occupation artefact, owner-counted). Side: only ENG (C.63), USA (C.72 + 2 C.10), SOV (8 C.10), GER (1), JAP (1) queued at all — ITA/FRA/RAJ 0 adds.
  - NOT YET TESTED — fix postdates every campaign in the registry.

### R54. UK air-basing gets throughput, and the ledger says who paid (Fix 87: P1 second lane slot, P6 nominal target; Fix 87b: hostile-held projects free their budget slot, UK budget 3 → 5 in deep deficit; P2 headroom shipped and removed the same day)

- **Opened 2026-08-16** from the `af003548` factory-allocation ledger (ENG and USA, 9 anchors 1941.6 → 1945.6). The UK hosting builder followed demand but delivered **~2 air-base levels/month** (36 → 105 levels 1941.6 → 1944.6, 130 by 1946.5) while the faction grew 8 500 → 14 000 planes; one level (500 + 300 × L ≈ 3 200–3 800 IC in 1944) is ~1 month of one 20-civ slot. Why so slow: ENG funded air at **20 civs** at 7 of 9 anchors (its pool 52–137, fully spent, `civs_avail = 0` in 1945, naval bases 40 and radar/refineries taking the rest); the USA funded 20–60 while holding **187–546 idle civs** at every anchor because `@AI_PC_ALLOC_FRACTION` 0.40 capped its pool at 100–154. And the target itself under-read the need: script counts planes, the engine books wing slots at `land_air_wing_size` (7 of 11 hosting states at exactly 100.0 % nominal on 1944.7 while reading 77 % in planes).
- **Fix under test:** Fix 87 + 87b in `WA_AI_CONSTRUCTION_PRIORITY_core.txt` / `_strategies.txt`. **P6** target = planes ÷ `@UK_AIR_NOMINAL_FILL` (0.9) and the deficit is published as `WA_AI_PC_air_deficit_pct` (state variable, 0 when none). **P1** a SECOND Fix 78 lane slot while `WA_AI_PC_air_deficit_pct > @AI_PC_AIR_LANE_2ND_SLOT_PCT` (20). **87b (a)** a project whose target state is ENEMY-controlled (Fix 34 frozen) no longer counts against `WA_AI_PC_start_project`'s per-type / per-strategy queue budget — it stays queued for recapture but stops holding a slot. **87b (b)** the UK hosting builder's concurrent-project budget is `@UK_AIR_QUEUE_MAX` 3 → `@UK_AIR_QUEUE_MAX_DEEP` 5 while the published deficit exceeds `@UK_AIR_DEEP_DEFICIT_PCT` (20). **P2 (headroom beyond the fraction cut) shipped 2026-08-16 in `cb1707f43` and was removed the same day** after the v17 local probe showed it drawing on ENG (civs_avail 2, pure vanilla-queue displacement) and never on the USA (civs_avail 267 — the USA never reaches its fraction cut for want of fundable projects); its `WA_TLM_r54_air_headroom_*` probes went with it. Probes (v17): `WA_TLM_r54_air_lane2_grants/_first_t/_last_t`.
- **Who pays, pre-registered from the ledger.** Inside the PC fill an extra 20-civ air slot displaces: ENG 1943.6 the two tail radars (17) + 3 civs of infrastructure; ENG 1944.6 the two funded naval bases (Alexandria 95 %, HK 51 % — nothing else is funded); ENG 1945 nothing (pool exhausted → refineries); USA the tail radar (12–14) + part of the next, every time. 87b (a)/(b) add no civs — they add concurrent UK PROJECTS (up to 5), so the fill order above applies to each extra funded project: the marginal victims are the same radars/infra (ENG 1943), naval bases (ENG 1944), USA radar. The v17 probe measured the pre-87b binder exactly: UK levels 106 → 112 in 3 months on cloud, v16 and v17 alike while the second slot funded 14 weeks and the USA held 267 idle civs.
- **Pass, four legs:**
  1. **Throughput.** UK hosting-state air-base levels (`airload.py --states 857,127,961,125,126,860,859,338,129,128,123`, or `buildings ENG --match air_base` on those states) rise by ≥ **3 levels/month** averaged over any 12-month window in which `wa_ai_pc_air_deficit_pct > 20` (baseline ~2), and capacity ≥ 90 % of `wa_ai_uk_air_dbg_planes ÷ 0.9` by the month before the first western landing (`af003548`: 10 700 vs 15 760 → 68 %).
  2. **Mechanism.** `wa_tlm_r54_air_lane2_grants > 0` with `_first_t` inside the first deficit > 20 window, on at least one of ENG/USA; AND `pc ENG/USA --match air_base` shows ≥ 4 concurrent `uk_air` projects on at least one builder in a save where `wa_ai_pc_air_deficit_pct > 20` (87b (b) opened the budget); AND no funded queue is headed by a 0-civ project on enemy-controlled ground while a same-type site could be queued (87b (a)).
  3. **Cost bounded — the fill.** No PC building type's cumulative completions (`wa_tlm_pc_built_by_type^N`) fall below its `af003548` reading at the same date by more than 20 %: rail (ENG 189 / USA 12 by 1944.6), naval (6 / 2), radar (9 / 33), refineries (2 / 0). A larger drop on radar is the accepted P4-shaped cost; a drop on rail or naval bases is NOT (they sit above the air band and should be untouched by the lanes).
  4. **Cost bounded — the vanilla queue.** With P2 removed no air civ comes from beyond the fraction cut, so `wa_tlm_pc_assigned` stays ≤ the fraction × `alloc_base` (+ the two Fix 41/78 lanes) on ENG and USA at every anchor, and the USA's synthetic-refinery park (`buildings USA --match synthetic`) does not lag `af003548`'s at the same date by more than 12 months (1 level 1943.6, 16 at 1944.6, 74 at 1946.5).
- **Probe:** `savegame.py tlm ENG <saves> --match "^wa_tlm_r54_|^wa_tlm_pc_civs|^wa_tlm_pc_avail|^wa_tlm_pc_assigned|^wa_tlm_pc_alloc"` and USA; `savegame.py var ENG "^wa_ai_pc_air_deficit_pct=|^wa_ai_uk_air_dbg_(capacity|planes)=" <monthly saves 1942.1→1945.6>` and USA; `airload.py --states <11 hosting ids> <saves>`; `pc ENG/USA <saves> --limit 0` for the funded order; `var TAG "^wa_tlm_pc_built_by_type\^"` for leg 3; `buildings USA --match synthetic` for leg 4. **Absence contract:** `wa_tlm_version < 17` = NOT YET TESTED.
- **Threshold:** 3 (levels are counting facts off two sweeps).
- **Regression watch:** (a) rail or naval completions dropping (leg 3) — the lanes are pulling from above their band, which the design says cannot happen: bug; (b) `wa_ai_pc_air_deficit_pct` reading > 20 with `r54_air_lane2_grants = 0` for 6+ months — no second type-2 project to fund even with the 5-project budget: the south-first walk found no buildable site (slots/cap), not the lane; (c) the second slot funding a `theatre_air` project outside Europe (it selects the first two type-2 projects above 250 in queue order — after Fix 85 that includes 350-band theatre projects, intended; a Pacific 300-band project taking the UK's second slot is not); (d) 87b (a) side effect: a builder re-queuing the SAME hostile-held state again because the frozen project no longer counts — `WA_AI_PC_has_same_type_project_here` still blocks it (per-state, not budget), so this should not happen; if `pc` shows two projects on one hostile state, the guard is being bypassed; (e) ENG's naval bases / refineries starving harder with 5 UK projects funded — that is the pre-registered fill cost, measured by leg 3, and the point at which `@UK_AIR_QUEUE_MAX_DEEP` should come down.
- **Deliberately NOT done, and why:** the cap (`@UK_AIR_TARGET_LEVEL_CAP` 12) and the 11-state list are untouched — at 1944.7 the hosting states sat at 105/132, so the cap was not the binder in 1944 (it becomes one from 1945.6); revisit as P3 once 87b has a campaign of readings. Radar (P4) is untouched too — pre-registered as the expected fill casualty, measured by leg 3, not decided. P2 is out for the reason above.
- **History:**
  - 2026-08-16 · `af003548` **local probe** (`ENG_1944_10_03_19.hoi4`, fork 1944.7.1 on the v17 build with Fixes 83/85/86/87/88; controls: cloud 1944.10 and the v16 local `USA_1944_10_01_11`) · **leg 1 FAIL, leg 2 partial, leg 3 PASS, leg 4 FAIL on the ENG/USA clause — not scored.** UK hosting levels **106 → 112 on all three paths, byte-identical per state (2.0/month)**: Fix 87 bought zero UK levels; capacity 11 200 vs `planes ÷ 0.9` 14 191 = 79 %. Mechanism: `r54_air_lane2_grants` ENG 14 / USA 13 from 1944.7 (pass) but `r54_air_headroom_civs` **ENG 25 (civs_avail 2), USA 0 (civs_avail 267)** — regression watch (a) verbatim: the USA never reaches its fraction cut (assigned 100 vs base 367 × 0.4) because it lacks fundable projects, so P2 can only ever draw on ENG, where it is pure displacement. Fill cost: no `pc_built_by_type` below cloud on any type; USA radar FUNDING 80 → 20 civs while USA total PC spend fell 140 → 100 with 267 civs idle. Why nothing moved: the second slot's ENG pick was a 350-band Normandy project (1016), the USA already funded its UK projects at 20 each on the cloud path, and **theatre_air projects on ENEMY-controlled states sit at the queue head holding type-2 budget slots** (USA 117 Campania RIT-controlled p350 0 civs stall 9; USA 785 Picardy GER-controlled; ENG 24 Centre GER-controlled stall 16) — starved correctly by the Fix 34 guard, but occupying the 3-per-builder budget, so no new UK site can be queued. **The binder is the concurrent-project budget (`@UK_AIR_QUEUE_MAX` 3 × ~1 month per level), not civs.** Fix 86 on this build: ENG idle 58 → 28 %, USA 79 → 47 %, escorts 160 → 341 / 29 → 207. Boot test clean (a mid-edit `WA_TLM_core.txt` reload at 1944.7.1 threw 36 transient errors, then `no errors`).
  - NOT YET TESTED — fix postdates every campaign in the registry (`af003548` is the diagnosing campaign; its ledger is the baseline for legs 3–4).

Durable rules belong in `wa-lessons-learned`; this ledger keeps the retirement evidence, the pending instrumentation dispositions, and the hand-offs that no live item carries. No instrumentation to retire - the probe is save-native.*

*__The "3 decks with no fighter wing" constant that held this item for one session is now fully explained, and it is NOT an AI defect.__ Named at last, from a two-pass `strategic_air` join on `02bd4445`: JAP decks **391 Ryujo, 392 Kaga, 393 Soryu, 394 Akagi, 395 Hosho**; USA decks **396 USS Ranger, 397 USS Lexington, 398 USS Saratoga**. Worked example, IJN Kaga at 1944.6 - `air_base` id 392, `carrier={ id=2072 }`, capacity 90, **nine `cv_nav_bomber` wings (ids 261-269, count=10 each) = 90/90 planes = 100.0% fill, cv_fighter 0**; at 1946.1 the same nine wing ids are still there, re-equipped within the archetype to `JAP_b6n_airframe` but **never re-roled** in ten game-years. Root cause is the **start-state OOB, not the AI**: `history/units/JAP_1936_air_bba_ai.txt` defines Kaga as nine consecutive `JAP_b4y_airframe` wings with no fighter wing at all (Ryujo 4, Hosho 2, Akagi 9, Soryu 7 the same), and `history/units/USA_1936_air_bba_ai.txt` does the same with `USA_bg_airframe` for Ranger/Lexington/Saratoga. **The BBA-branch OOB files contain ZERO carrier-fighter entries; their `_legacy` twins do contain them.** The decisive contrast: at 1938.6 the two carriers JAP had *built itself* (decks 430 and 433, both Hiryu) already carried **40 cv_fighter each** - the AI composes every hull it produces on a 4:3 fighter/bomber split and simply never revisits the ones it was handed. Material was never the constraint: 167 carrier fighters in JAP stock and **3 200 more in land-based wings** at 1946.1 (2 114 of them A6M-family), and 71 in stock as early as 1938.6.*

*__Correction of record, and it inverts a claim this item carried: "fill ~100%, zero empty decks" is true for JAP and FALSE for the USA.__ JAP is exactly at capacity - 1 380 / 1 380 across all 20 decks at both 1944.6 and 1946.1. The USA at 1944.6 is **2 019 / 2 720 = 74.2%**, because **11 Essex-class decks sit at exactly 50/100**, each carrying five `cv_nav_bomber` wings with `count = 0` - **55 empty wings**, though no deck is wholly empty. The cause is a shortage, not an assignment failure: **14 `cv_nav_bomber` airframes in USA stock at 1944.6**. Aggregating fill across decks is what hid this for five campaigns; any future carrier probe must report per-deck, not per-country. Also worth recording: **zero `cv_cas` planes exist on any JAP or USA deck at any sampled date** - the `cv_small_bomber_airframe` archetype is entirely unused afloat.*

*__Two defects handed off, neither of them what this item tested__ (protocol: a pass that reveals a new defect does not get absorbed into the item). (i) **The BBA carrier OOB gap** - eight start-state hulls across JAP and USA with no fighter wing, stable for ten game-years; it is start-state data, small and verifiable in the first save of any campaign. (ii) **The USA carrier naval-bomber shortage** - 11 half-empty decks against 14 airframes in stock. Note (ii) points the opposite way from the carrier-aircraft **over**-production finding recorded against JAP/ENG/FRA in the anomalies list below: Japan holds 740 naval bombers afloat and ~7 000 carrier aircraft parked ashore while the USA cannot fill its decks, so **any global cut to carrier-aircraft production would hit the one navy that is short**. Whoever picks up either defect must read both together.*
