Dalmatia, 33 Centre-Sud) — no home-soil spree.*

***Instrumentation disposition — PROMOTE, do not delete, and this is a deliberate departure from the default.** The `WA_AI_thair_dbg_*` family fails the standing-vs-probe test in `documentation/WA_TLM_TELEMETRY_SYSTEM.md` §3.8 on the "was it used beyond this item's own pass/fail" clause: the triple `called` / `started` / `active` is the **only** way any of the four defects below is visible, all four are still open, and `_started` additionally serves as the documented reference implementation of the queue-growth guard that R8's `uk_air` twin lacks (§2.1). Deleting it would make four live defects undiagnosable and remove the only correct counter of its class. Decision: **keep the write sites, promote the family to a standing `WA_TLM_thair_*` name with its own registry row**, as a separate commit with a boot test — the same disposition and the same reasoning R14's `_active` received, and for the same reason it was not executed inline (it edits construction-priority gameplay files immediately before a campaign, where a parse error costs a multi-hour cloud run for no analytical gain). The item is retired now; the rename is owed.*

***Four unresolved defects carried out of R16 — they are NOT fixed, they simply were never this item's pass criterion.** (1) **The JAP builder is dead**: 434 calls, 4 starts, `active` = 0 from 1943.6, so the Axis Pacific side builds nothing all war — 4th campaign running. (2) **The IPP Philippine group 623-628 is still flat** (623 = 1, 624-628 = 0) for the 5th campaign; Fix 38's host-is-funding gate demonstrably does not reach occupied-subject territory. (3) **327 Manila reaches air-base level 5, above the 4-cap**, at 1944.8 and holds it — third instance of the pattern, and the subject-owned-state ladder-exclusion hypothesis is now the standing explanation. (4) The overproduction guard is breached again at **CAN 32 vs USA 58 = 55%**, which is not the "small fraction" the guard expects; the guard has never once failed an item in five campaigns, so give it a numeric bound or drop it. Any of these deserves its own item if it is to be fixed.*

*Retired 2026-08-13 (streak 3/3, `02bd4445`): **R20** (warbond ladder recovers from the fatigue-0 dead-end, Fix 35 `a03fc502b`). Final pass: **JAP escapes the dead-end and climbs** Series_B (1938.6) -> D (1939.6) -> E (1940.6) -> F (1942.6), holding F to 1946.1 at 150 owned / 174 controlled `industrial_complex` against Series_G's `>200` civilian-factory rung -- i.e. parked by the ladder's own size gate, which is the design, not the `31eaf7e6` pathology (`economic_fatigue` 46 -> 95 with no steel collapse). Criterion (2) satisfied on six tags (`wa_warbonds_retry_upgrades` >= 1 on JAP, CHI, ITA, USA, FRA, ROM); criterion (3) clean (GER Series_G by 1941.6, ENG by 1942.6). Criterion (4) degraded -- JAP's steel park runs 21:4 -> 25:0 -> **0:25** from 1944.6 -- but the driver is **iron** (net +47 at 1943.6 -> -53 at 1944.6, steel net still +103), so it is booked to R29, not here.*

*__The blocker that held this item for a campaign was a PROBE ARTEFACT, not a defect, and the durable lesson is worth more than the item was.__ The "ENG bond ladder resets: Series_G -> no bond idea at all -> Series_A", flagged as an anomaly-to-confirm across three consecutive campaigns, is neither a reset nor a loss. Repaying Series_G runs `swap_ideas = { remove_idea = Series_G_bonds add_idea = No_bonds }` in both the `complete_effect` and the `timeout_effect` of `common/decisions/_warbond_payback_decisions.txt:38` and `:49` (every rung has the same pair). The terminal state is the **`No_bonds` idea**, a real idea the country genuinely holds -- verified directly on `02bd4445`: ENG reads `Series_G_bonds` at 1944.6 and **`No_bonds` at 1945.9 and 1946.1**. The item's own probe is `ideas <TAG> --match "Series_"`, which cannot match `No_bonds`, so it reported "no bond idea at all" three campaigns running. The later `Series_A` sighting on `9a4cd657` is the ladder legitimately re-entering after repayment, not a regression. **Rule for any future warbond probe: match `bonds|No_bonds`, never `Series_` alone.** Reaching Series_G and having nowhere further to go is **intended** (user decision, 2026-08-13).*

*__Instrumentation disposition: DELETE, deferred to a follow-up boot-tested commit.__ `wa_warbonds_retry_upgrades` has exactly one write site -- a bare `add_to_variable` at `common/decisions/_economy_fatigue.txt:3348`, with no zero-init, so absence means "never incremented" rather than "not instrumented". It fails the standing-vs-probe promotion test of `documentation/WA_TLM_TELEMETRY_SYSTEM.md` §3.8: it was only ever criterion (2) of this item and of the one-shot migration sub-probe below, and no analysis used it for anything else. Not removed inline because it edits a gameplay decisions file and therefore owes an F9 boot test -- same deferral, and the same reason, as R14's `_active` and R1's `AIFC-DIAG` lines.*

*__The migration sub-probe retires PASSED and does not need re-running.__ `WA_AI_warbonds_migration_1` (`common/scripted_effects/WA_AI_MIGRATION_effects.txt`, console-invoked by design) was verified exhaustively on `cbca536d`: 34 countries holding `Series_A..F`, **26 armed, 0 false positives**, and all 8 declines individually explained (2 carrying `issuing_war_bonds`, 6 owning zero states). It exists only to repair saves that dead-ended on a pre-Fix-35 build; from-start campaigns do not need it. Keep the effect for save repair, or delete it with the counter above -- either is defensible now that the fix itself is retired.*

*Retired 2026-08-13 (streak 5/5, `02bd4445`): **R26** (PC overtake lane serves starved projects; revalidation filters, Fix 41 `f4ef2b059`). Final pass: ENG `wa_tlm_pc_aging_grants` **49** against `_reval_cancels` **5** - a ~10x serve:cull ratio, the best recorded - with GER 71/147 and SOV 79/81. Livelock broken (ENG holds live type-8 and type-15 refineries in every sampled queue, synthetic refineries 2 -> 6, UK air capacity 5 200 -> 11 100). Max `stall_weeks` on funded countries: ENG 7 / 7 / 13, USA 13, inside the ~22 bar. Criterion (5) on the rail-heavy recut: SOV 1944.6 = **41 of 43 projects type-13 (95%)**.*

*__The blocker that held this item - `wa_tlm_pc_aging_grants` = 0 on the USA for four consecutive campaigns - is DIAGNOSED and is correct behaviour on both counts. Do not re-open it.__ Two facts stacked:*

*__(a) The USA queue is essentially never starved.__ The lane only serves projects stalled >= 12 weeks, and the USA funds its queue at the per-project cap of 20 almost everywhere: `assigned_factories_total` / `queue^num` reads 5/100, 12/200, 9/180, 6/120, 7/140 ... 5/100 across 23 sampled saves 1941.3 -> 1946.1, so `stall_weeks` is 0 on virtually every slot. ENG - the country that grants 49 times - is the opposite, chronically below `20 x N`: 6/96, 6/57, 8/42, 5/32, 5/32 over 1942.6 -> 1946.1. That contrast alone accounts for most of the 49-vs-0.*

*__(b) Every USA project that DID stall >= 12 weeks was hostile-frozen, which the lane refuses by design.__ Only four project-episodes crossed 12 weeks in the whole campaign, all `type_id = 21` theatre air bases (Fix 47) on enemy-controlled ground: state 288 Burma (RAJ -> JAP, stall 20), 334 Kalimantan (INS -> JAP, 23), 851 Pirot (YUG -> BUL, 13), 1031 Siem Reap (FRV -> JAP, 17). The rejecting term is the Fix 34 friendly-controller test inside the lane's candidate scan, `common/scripted_effects/WA_AI_CONSTRUCTION_PRIORITY_core.txt:456-465`, and it is deliberate and documented at `:414-417`: "a project frozen on hostile-controlled ground (Fix 34) is NOT lane material - it is deliberately left to recapture-or-sweep, not cancelled early." **Decisive in-save proof it is the controller term and not pool exhaustion:** at 1942.6 the priority-sorted queue is `[5, 3, 1, 4, 2]` and the fill gives **0 factories to slots 5 and 3 at the HEAD** while paying 20 each to 1, 4 and 2 below them. The clean control is in the same family - 1944.9 project 2 targets state 33 (GER-owned, **FRA-controlled**, i.e. allied) and is funded 20 at stall 0.*

*__Durable rule worth more than the item: `grants = 0` AND `reval_cancels = 0` together are the signature of "the candidate scan never yielded a candidate", not of a broken lane.__ The controller rejection happens in the scan, **before** `WA_AI_PC_project_still_relevant` runs, so neither counter can fire. Any future zero on this pair should be read that way first. The structural reason it is the USA specifically, four campaigns running: its PC queue is dominated by Fix 47 theatre air bases aimed at forward theatre states, which are by construction the states most likely to be enemy-held - the USA's entire stall population IS the population the lane refuses.*

*__Instrumentation disposition: DELETE, deferred to a follow-up commit.__ `WA_TLM_pc_aging_grants` / `_reval_cancels` fail the standing-vs-probe promotion test of `documentation/WA_TLM_TELEMETRY_SYSTEM.md` §3.8 - they were R26's pass criteria and nothing else, and the one investigation that used them beyond pass/fail (the USA zero) is now closed. Four sites plus two registry rows: `WA_AI_CONSTRUCTION_PRIORITY_core.txt:495` and `:529` (write), `WA_TLM_core.txt:68-69` (zero-init), `WA_TLM_TELEMETRY_SYSTEM.md:302-303` (registry). **Not done inline because a parallel session holds `WA_TLM_core.txt` open at the time of writing**, and because it edits construction-priority gameplay files and therefore owes an F9 boot test - the same deferral, and the same reason, as R14's `_active`, R1's `AIFC-DIAG` lines and R20's `wa_warbonds_retry_upgrades`. **Do NOT add `_first_t`/`_last_t` stamps before deleting**: they would not have answered the USA question (the zero was already provably genuine via the `WA_TLM_core.txt:61-62` zero-init, and the diagnosis needed queue-level `target_state` + controller state, which no stamp carries). If this lane is ever instrumented again, the useful metric is a **rejection-reason counter on the scan**, not a timestamp on the grant.*

*__Two findings handed off, neither owned by this item:__ (i) hostile-frozen USA projects hold a queue slot for 5-6 months before the week-25 sweep (stall 10 -> 14 -> 19 -> 23 across 1942.4 -> 1942.7) and are counted by `WA_AI_PC_active_projects` and the non-rail `< 5` strategy gate while frozen - that belongs to **R19 / Fix 34**. (ii) The diagnosis was run only on `02bd4445`; the mechanism is structural and should recur, but the USA zeros of `911bed3c` / `9a4cd657` / `a8b29e09` were not re-opened to confirm they share this exact cause. Cheap check if ever wanted: `var USA "^wa_ai_pc_(stall_weeks|target_state|assigned_factories)"` on two mid-1942 saves of one earlier campaign.*

Also note `posture_vs_ger` never reaches level 2 after 1944.4: `WA_TLM_post_grind_n` is **20 for ENG and 9 for USA against 4 867 / 4 126 level-1 observations**, so the careful/grind mode is effectively dead code in a real campaign.
